The framework translates regulatory information into (1) formalized checklists and executable decision trees, encoding regulations and conditions as interpretable code structures. During inference, each checklist item is (2) dynamically expanded into factual and counterfactual questions, guiding the model to reason over case-specific evidence and potential violations. This process establishes a reasoning pipeline that proceeds from evidence identification, through rule application, to final decision-making, while a self-verification loop improves the logical consistency of the generated code and the traceability of outcomes. Experiments on privacy and data protection scenarios demonstrate that Code-as-Auditor delivers more accurate and evidence-backed evaluations, enabling automated compliance regulation checking grounded in explicit regulatory criteria.

</details>


### [181] [DeltaSelect: Affordable A/B Testing for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19607)
*Nicholas J. Conn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文针对编码智能体基准测试不适合高频开发决策的问题，提出开源方法 DeltaSelect，用于在预算内挑选能稳定反映全基准表现的子任务集，并在一个案例研究中验证其可用性与成本优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准面向广泛、全面的模型比较，而非频繁的开发决策：单次运行结果波动大、完整套件开销高昂，且基准测试框架可能与实际使用的框架不一致，导致结果难以指导开发迭代。

Method: 对 DeepSWE 已公布试验进行重采样分析，用皮尔逊相关衡量任务单次运行结果与全基准表现的一致性；基于相关系数筛选任务；用线性回归将分数型验证器结果映射到统一分数；在给定美元预算下选择固定任务集，形成 DeltaSelect。

Result: 重采样显示仅 19.5%（113 个中 22 个）任务与全基准表现达到至少 0.50 的第五百分位皮尔逊相关。在 gpt-5.6-luna 低推理案例研究中，13 次评估共花费 USD 27.86（按 2026-08-16 公布费率）；采纳版本比初始版本便宜 58.1%（USD 1.75 vs 4.18，p=0.008），校准分数更高（42.36% vs 36.46%；按公布数据模拟方差 p=0.326）。

Conclusion: DeltaSelect 定位于开发过程中反复进行的基线-候选对比，而非模型排名；它能在预算约束下提供与全基准一致的任务子集，并通过案例研究证明其在改进自定义技能与指令时可显著降低成本，但分数提升的统计显著性尚不足。

Abstract: Coding-agent benchmarks are built for broad and comprehensive comparisons, not frequent development decisions. Individual runs vary, full suites are expensive, and the benchmark harness may differ from the harness used in practice. In a resampling analysis of DeepSWE's published trials, only 19.5% of tasks (22 of 113) had a fifth-percentile Pearson correlation of at least 0.50 with full-benchmark performance. The paper presents DeltaSelect, an open-source method that identifies tasks whose one-run results consistently track full-benchmark performance using Pearson correlation, maps fractional verifier results to a common score using linear regression, and selects a fixed task set within a dollar budget. DeltaSelect is intended for repeated baseline-versus-candidate comparisons during development, not model rankings. In a gpt-5.6-luna low-reasoning case study, DeltaSelect was used to revise custom skills and instructions. Across 13 evaluations, the recorded cost was USD 27.86 at rates published August 16, 2026. The adopted version cost 58.1% less than the initial version (USD 1.75 versus USD 4.18; p=0.008), while the calibrated score was higher (42.36% versus 36.46%; published-analog variance p=0.326).

</details>


### [182] [BA-TRACE: Boundary-Aware Trace Reconstruction for Scenario-Based Evaluation of Mixed AUTOSAR Adaptive and ROS 2 Vehicular Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19699)
*Shunsuke Ito,Ryudai Iwakami,Hiroyuki Hanyu,Tasuku Ishigooka,Takuya Azumi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BA-TRACE是一个边界感知的追踪重构框架，用于混合AUTOSAR AP与ROS 2车载嵌入式系统的场景化评估，通过融合多源事件重建跨DDS-SOME/IP边界的端到端执行图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合栈（ROS 2自动驾驶栈+AUTOSAR AP）中执行路径跨越DDS-SOME/IP中间件边界，现有模拟器与追踪工具只能执行场景或采集平台内局部追踪，无法重建跨域数据流，导致场景化评估难以解读。

Method: BA-TRACE结合四类信息：ROS 2追踪事件、AUTOSAR ara::log事件、ARXML推导的结构依赖、桥接层插桩，跨DDS-SOME/IP边界重建端到端执行图。

Result: 基于AWSIM/OpenSCENARIO的目标检测与制动场景案例研究表明，BA-TRACE能重建预期的跨平台路径，并揭示边界特有的延迟（如点云传输开销）。

Conclusion: 重构的拓扑作为可追溯性证据，而非行为正确性或安全性的证明。

Abstract: Modern vehicular embedded systems increasingly combine ROS 2-based autonomous-driving stacks with AUTOSAR Adaptive Platform (AUTOSAR AP). Such mixed stacks make scenario-based evaluation hard to interpret because execution paths cross DDS-SOME/IP middleware boundaries between ROS 2 and AUTOSAR AP. Existing simulators and tracing tools execute scenarios or collect platform-local traces but cannot reconstruct cross-domain data flows. This paper presents BA-TRACE, a boundary-aware trace reconstruction framework for scenario-based evaluation of mixed AUTOSAR AP and ROS 2 vehicular embedded systems. BA-TRACE combines ROS 2 trace events, AUTOSAR ara::log events, ARXML-derived structural dependencies, and bridge-level instrumentation to reconstruct an end-to-end execution graph across the DDS-SOME/IP boundary. A case study with an AWSIM/OpenSCENARIO-based object-detection and braking scenario shows that BA-TRACE reconstructs the expected cross-platform path and exposes boundary-specific latency such as point-cloud transfer overhead. The reconstructed topology is used as evidence of traceability, not as proof of behavioral correctness or safety.

</details>


### [183] [A Closed-Loop Control Architecture for Reliable Constraint Satisfaction in LLM Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19710)
*Quan Zhou,Shahbaz Siddeeq,Mika Saari,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出并评估一种五阶段闭环控制架构，用于让大语言模型在可编程检验的数值约束（如词数、可读性等级）下稳定产出文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统中越来越多地嵌入大语言模型，并需满足可用代码检验的数值输出约束；但模型非确定性、依赖自然语言指令、且只能近似满足要求，单次提示既不可靠达标，也难以保留原文内容。

Method: 采用闭环控制架构，含生成、评估、调整、归档、分析五阶段。模型仅负责写与改，确定性代码负责比较复合可读性值与目标区间、拒绝丢失原文实体/数字/关键词的编辑，并做全部接受决策。

Result: 在四个商用模型上，114次单次生成中达标率为21.1%–31.6%，240次闭环运行达标率为92.5%–98.8%；平均两轮编辑内收敛，基于召回的内容保真度为0.92–0.93；两个共同模型的对比显示同样效果。

Conclusion: 由于控制器优化的是评分所用指标，结果证明的是对已声明、可计算指标的可复现控制，而非已验证的人类难度。可迁移实践是：将接受条件写成代码、将模型限制为局部编辑、并对每次编辑做内容检查。

Abstract: Software systems increasingly embed a large language model in features that must satisfy a numeric output constraint, that is, a requirement expressible as a number or an interval and checkable by code, such as a target word count or a target readability grade band. Because such a model is non-deterministic, is configured through natural-language instructions rather than a typed interface, and satisfies a stated requirement only approximately, a single prompt neither reliably meets the target nor preserves the source content. This paper presents and evaluates a closed-loop control architecture for this problem. It has five stages: generate, evaluate, adjust, archive, and analyze. The model is called only to write and to edit text, while deterministic code compares a composite readability value against a target band, rejects any edit that drops source entities, numbers, or keywords, and makes every accept decision. Over 114 single-shot generation jobs and 240 closed-loop runs on four commercial models, single-shot prompting met the target in 21.1 to 31.6 percent of cases and the closed loop in 92.5 to 98.8 percent, within two edit rounds on average and at a recall-based fidelity of 0.92 to 0.93; the two models common to both settings show the same effect. Because the controller optimizes the value on which success is scored, the result establishes reproducible control over a declared, computable metric and not validated human difficulty. The transferable practice is to declare the acceptance condition as code, bound the model to local edits, and gate every edit on a content check.

</details>


### [184] [EviRCA: Decoupling Evidence Extraction from Reasoning for Microservice Root-Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19825)
*Yuhao Wang,Zhen Qin,Xingliang Wang,Guochang Li,Weize Li,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EviRCA框架将确定性证据提取与LLM推理解耦，把原始遥测转为结构化证据卡，显著提升微服务根因分析准确率并大幅降低开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代微服务系统的根因分析（RCA）耗时且依赖人力，虽有LLM智能体尝试解决，但让单一模型同时检索证据、定位故障并推断根因，存在计算成本高、行为不稳定、诊断精度有限的问题；且原始遥测（数值指标、结构化追踪、机器日志）不适合LLM直接处理。

Method: 提出EviRCA框架，将确定性证据提取与LLM推理解耦。系统无关的提取阶段把原始指标、追踪和日志转换为紧凑、忠实的多模态证据卡；LLM仅通过一小组预定义的只读工具在这些结构化观测上推理，不访问原始遥测也不执行代码。

Result: 在基于三个企业系统真实异构遥测构建的OpenRCA基准上，EviRCA在两个不同LLM上达到40.6%-43.9%的正确率，远超此前最高15.2%的基线；token消耗降低15-26倍，执行时间减少3-20倍，并能解决此前近乎零性能的需同时推理时间、组件与根因的难题。

Conclusion: 过程级失败分析表明瓶颈在于判断已提取证据而非搜索证据，说明LLM根因分析的效果高度依赖证据提取质量。

Abstract: Root-cause analysis (RCA) is a critical yet labor-intensive task for maintaining modern microservice systems, making it an attractive target for large language models (LLMs). Recent agentic approaches allow an LLM to iteratively explore raw telemetry by generating and executing code, asking a single model to simultaneously retrieve evidence, localize faults, and infer root causes over large volumes of heterogeneous telemetry, which leads to high computational cost, unstable behavior, and limited diagnostic accuracy. However, raw telemetry consists of numeric metrics, structured traces, and machine-generated logs that are not directly suitable for LLM processing. We present EviRCA, a framework for LLM-based RCA that decouples deterministic evidence extraction from LLM reasoning. A system-agnostic extraction stage converts raw metrics, traces, and logs into a compact set of faithful multimodal evidence cards, while the LLM reasons only over these structured observations through a small set of predefined read-only tools, without accessing raw telemetry or executing code. We evaluate EviRCA on OpenRCA, a benchmark built from real, heterogeneous telemetry across three enterprise systems. EviRCA achieves a correct rate of 40.6%-43.9% across two different LLMs, substantially outperforming prior OpenRCA baselines that achieve up to 15.2%, while reducing token consumption by 15-26x and execution time by 3-20x. Moreover, EviRCA solves hard cases requiring simultaneous reasoning over time, components, and root causes, a setting where previous approaches reported near-zero performance. Our process-level failure analysis further shows that the bottleneck lies in judging the evidence that the extraction stage has already surfaced, rather than searching for it, suggesting that the effectiveness of LLM-based RCA depends heavily on the quality of evidence extraction.

</details>


### [185] [HapCiD: Detecting API-related Compatibility Issues in OpenHarmony Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20099)
*Daihang Chen,Yonghui Liu,Mingyi Zhou,Li Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出开源工具HapCiD，用行为模型自动检测OpenHarmony应用的API兼容性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenHarmony平台快速演进，SDK不断引入新API导致兼容性问题（类似Android），但现有工具因ArkTS新语言及缺乏成熟行为模型而失效。

Method: 构建HapCiD开源工具，基于行为模型捕捉应用执行语义，自动检测API相关兼容性问题。

Result: 在4,478个应用中检测出163个应用共2,040个兼容性问题，准确率达100%。

Conclusion: 系统研究兼容性问题，对其类型分类，分析现有缓解策略并提出未来改进方向。

Abstract: OpenHarmony, an emerging open-source mobile platform, is rapidly gaining attention in the mobile development community. Its fast-evolving Software Development Kit (SDK) introduces numerous Application Programming Interfaces (APIs) to boost developer productivity but inevitably introduces compatibility challenges, an issue well known from platforms like Android. However, existing compatibility analysis tools are ineffective for OpenHarmony due to its newly introduced ArkTS programming language and the lack of a mature behavioral model to capture app execution semantics. To bridge this gap, we present HapCiD, an open-source tool for automatically detecting API-related compatibility issues in OpenHarmony apps. Applied to 4,478 apps, HapCiD successfully detects 2,040 compatibility issues across 163 apps with 100 percent accuracy. Based on these findings, we conduct a comprehensive study of compatibility issues, categorizing their types, examining existing mitigation strategies, and proposing future improvements.

</details>


### [186] [AdaRepair-Mem: Adaptive Experience Orchestration for Repository-Level Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20130)
*Z. C. Luo,J. C. Guo,W. J. He,S. Y. Wang,J. C. Yu,F. M. Zhao,Y. Chen,T. Cao,L. Q. Liu,N. Zheng,W. Xu,J. Jiang,Z. M. Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出自适应经验检索框架用于仓库级程序修复，通过覆盖感知检索、质量感知选择和阶段感知路由解决记忆检索的三个局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级记忆增强程序修复依赖复用历史修复经验，但存在三个问题：(1) episodic memory跨仓库分布极不平衡，低资源仓库获得的有效支持很少；(2) 更多记忆并不单调提升修复成功率，相关性、质量和冗余度比记忆数量更关键；(3) 记忆积累存在阶段错位，仓库可能有很多复现经验却缺乏补丁或精炼经验。

Method: 框架包含三个组件：(1) coverage-aware retrieval：当同仓库记忆不足时回退到跨仓库或按修复类型的记忆；(2) quality-aware selection：按相关性、历史效用、具体性和冗余度对记忆排序；(3) stage-aware routing：为复现、定位、补丁生成、补丁精炼和验证分别分离并检索记忆。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Lite和SWE-Bench-Verified上评估，该框架提升了覆盖不足仓库的修复性能，减少了噪声记忆检索，并更好地支持failed-to-fixed补丁精炼。

Conclusion: 记忆增强修复的关键不是简单积累更多经验，而是为正确的修复上下文检索正确的经验。

Abstract: Recent memory-augmented repository-level program repair methods reuse historical repair experiences to improve LLM-based issue resolution. However, our analysis reveals three limitations in existing repository-level memory retrieval. First, episodic memory is highly imbalanced across repositories, leaving low-resource repositories with little effective support. Second, more memory does not monotonically lead to higher repair success, suggesting that relevance, quality, and redundancy matter more than raw memory volume. Third, memory accumulation is phase-misaligned: repositories may contain many reproduction experiences but few patch or refinement experiences. To address these problems, we propose an adaptive experience retrieval framework for repository-level program repair. Our framework introduces coverage-aware retrieval, which falls back to cross-repository or repair-type-based memories when same-repository memory is insufficient; quality-aware selection, which ranks memories by relevance, historical utility, specificity, and redundancy; and stage-aware routing, which separates and retrieves memories for reproduction, localization, patch generation, patch refinement, and validation. Evaluated on SWE-Bench-Lite and SWE-Bench-Verified, the proposed framework improves repair performance on under-covered repositories, reduces noisy memory retrieval, and better supports failed-to-fixed patch refinement. Our results show that the key to memory-augmented repair is not simply accumulating more experiences, but retrieving the right experiences for the right repair context.

</details>


### [187] [CodeTransBenchmark: Evaluating LLM-based Code Translation and Repair Across Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20257)
*Vera Kowalczuk,Oliver Weißl,Severin Kacianka,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 CodeTransBenchmark 框架，评估 LLM 在代码翻译及翻译错误修复中的效果，发现专用多语言模型（如 Codestral）表现较好，而通用模型常难以掌握目标语言语法，迭代修复可显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在代码生成上展现潜力，其在代码翻译领域也受到关注，但 LLM 代码翻译及错误修复的有效性尚不明确，需要系统性评估。

Method: 提出 CodeTransBenchmark 评估框架，设计后处理策略从不一致的 LLM 输出中提取代码；对 8 个模型在 3 个数据集、12 个语言对上开展实证研究，将错误翻译按错误类型分类，并评估基于自动反馈的迭代翻译修复。

Result: Codestral 等专用多语言模型能正确翻译大部分代码，而多数通用模型难以掌握目标语言语法；错误分析显示编程语言间关系及训练数据对效果影响巨大；通用后处理需容忍不一致并利用 LLM 答案的可预测性；迭代修复显著提升翻译准确率。

Conclusion: LLM 在自动化代码翻译上具潜力，但实际部署需要具备更大上下文窗口的模型。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) pre-trained on expansive text and code corpora have revealed promising code generation abilities and have attracted increasing attention in code translation. In this work, we investigate the effectiveness of LLMs in code translation and translation error repair. First, we present CodeTransBenchmark, a framework for evaluating LLM-based translation and repair and devise a post-processing strategy to extract code from inconsistent LLM outputs. Then, we discuss an empirical study evaluates eight models on three datasets and 12 language pairs, in which we categorize incorrect translations by errors to identify weaknesses of existing LLMs. Our work shows that while LLMs specifically trained for multi-lingual coding, like Codestral, correctly translate the majority of code, most general-purpose models struggle with the syntactic rules of the target language. The analysis of erroneous translations reveals the substantial impact of the interrelationship between involved programming languages and training data on the effectiveness. We show that a general post-processing approach must tolerate inconsistencies and leverage the predictability of LLM answers. Further, we show that iterative translation repair via automated feedback significantly improves translation accuracy. While our combined findings highlight the potential of LLMs to automate code translation, an effective deployment of LLM-based code translation in practice would require models with larger context windows.

</details>


### [188] [Evaluating Package-Level Scoping Strategies for Repository-Level Code Completion in Pharo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20279)
*Omar Abedelkader,Stéphane Ducasse,Guillermo Polito,Oleksandr Zaitsev,Romain Robbes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文为Pharo语义补全引擎提出三种包感知启发式（Lateral Packages、Direct Package Dependencies、Two-Level Package Dependencies），在219个包的基准上评估，发现Direct Package Dependencies效果最强，被Pharo 13采纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码以仓库、包、项目组织结构存在，但token级补全引擎很少利用这种结构。Pharo现有补全引擎基于有序惰性启发式，遵循语言语义与作用域规则，却忽略包结构和依赖，导致同包/同项目类及其被引用类无法获得优先排序。

Method: 提出三种包感知启发式：Lateral Packages、Direct Package Dependencies、Two-Level Package Dependencies。基准覆盖219个包的方法调用点，含4,535个类、35,972个方法，来自Iceberg、Moose、Roassal、Seaside、Spec等主要Pharo项目。评估类名与方法名补全，检验显式包依赖能否作为轻量结构信号改进候选排序，而不引入新学习模型。

Result: Direct Package Dependencies启发式效果最强。对3字符前缀，类名补全的MRR从0.20升至0.44，方法名补全的MRR从0.10升至0.41。

Conclusion: 包感知策略在所评估项目上比默认的Semantics-Based策略提供更准确、更相关的建议。基于类名补全的改进，Pharo工业联盟已将Direct Package Dependencies启发式采纳用于Pharo 13。

Abstract: Source code is organized in repositories, packages, and projects, yet token-level completion engines rarely exploit this structure. Pharo provides a semantic completion engine based on ordered, lazy heuristics that retrieve and rank candidates. Its default strategy follows language semantics and scoping rules, but ignores package structure and dependencies. Consequently, classes from the same package or project, and classes used by them, receive no special priority.
  We introduce and evaluate three package-aware heuristics: Lateral Packages, Direct Package Dependencies, and Two-Level Package Dependencies. Our benchmark covers method call sites from 219 packages, including 4,535 classes and 35,972 methods across major Pharo projects such as Iceberg, Moose, Roassal, Seaside, and Spec. We assess class-name and method-name completion and ask whether explicit package dependencies can provide a lightweight structural signal for improving candidate ranking without introducing a new learning model.
  The Direct Package Dependencies heuristic produced the strongest results. For 3-character prefixes, Mean Reciprocal Rank increased from 0.20 to 0.44 for class-name completion and from 0.10 to 0.41 for method-name completion. These findings show that package-aware strategies provide more accurate and relevant suggestions than the default Semantics-Based strategy on the evaluated projects. Based on the improvement in class-name completion, the Pharo Industrial Consortium adopted the Direct Package Dependencies heuristic for Pharo 13.

</details>


### [189] [Towards a Characterization of Microservice Architectures Generated by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20308)
*José Renan,Ademar Sousa,Emanuel Dantas,Danyllo Albuquerque,Mirko Perkusic,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控实验，考察LLM从模块化单体描述生成微服务架构的结构与描述特征，发现提示策略（而非模型供应商）是主要影响因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益用于软件设计，但其生成架构的结构与一致性了解不足，尤其架构到架构（A2A）转换行为缺乏研究。

Method: 对两个模块化单体系统进行受控实验，比较zero-shot与few-shot提示策略，使用OpenAI GPT与DeepSeek模型；生成架构以标准化CSV表示，并用归一化指标评估服务粒度、服务间通信模式、通信密度、服务隔离与职责描述性。

Result: 差异主要由提示策略而非模型供应商驱动：few-shot持续产生更细粒度分解、更低通信密度和更详细职责描述；zero-shot倾向更粗粒度、更紧耦合的架构。

Conclusion: 研究不评估架构质量，而为理解文本驱动的架构到架构转换中LLM行为提供经验基线，支撑生成式设计工作流与早期软件现代化中混合架构推理的后续研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for software design tasks, yet little is known about the structure and coherence of the architectures they produce. In particular, how LLMs behave in Architecture-to-Architecture transformations remains insufficiently understood. This study empirically characterizes how LLMs generate microservice architectures from natural language descriptions of modular monoliths, focusing on structural and descriptive properties rather than architectural correctness. We conduct controlled experiments on two modular monolith systems, comparing zero-shot and few-shot prompting strategies using GPT-based models from OpenAI and models from DeepSeek. Generated architectures are represented in a standardized CSV format and evaluated using normalized metrics capturing service granularity, inter-service communication patterns, communication density, service isolation, and responsibility descriptiveness. The results reveal systematic differences driven primarily by prompting strategy rather than model provider. Few-shot prompting consistently produces finer-grained decompositions with lower communication density and more detailed responsibility descriptions, whereas zero-shot prompting favors coarser and more tightly connected architectures. Rather than assessing architectural quality, this study provides an empirical baseline for understanding LLM behavior in text-driven Architecture-to-Architecture transformations, informing future research on generative design workflows and hybrid architectural reasoning in early-stage software modernization.

</details>


### [190] [Assessing the Construct Validity of Object-Oriented, Class-Level Code Quality Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20411)
*Hera Arif,Miikka Kuutila,Paul Ralph*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文运用现代测量理论与因子分析方法，验证面向对象类级代码质量指标的构念效度，最终保留24个指标对应Size、Cohesion、Inheritance(含子/父继承)、Coupling(含入/出耦合)等构念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已提出并使用大量代码质量指标，但其构念效度很少被评估，导致这些指标是否真正测量了其声称测量的属性常常不明确。

Method: 从Apache Maven项目提取Designite、JHawk、Understand三工具测得的类级OO指标，用探索性因子分析(EFA)识别因子结构，并在22个随机选取的开源项目上用验证性因子分析(CFA)加以验证。

Result: EFA揭示出对应六个构念(Cohesion、In-Coupling、Out-Coupling、Size、Sub-Inheritance、Sup-Inheritance)的24个指标；10个指标不对应任何已知质量维度而被移除，另有10个在CFA中载荷过低建议移除。最终保留Size、Cohesion、Inheritance与Coupling四个构念，其中后两者含子类别。

Conclusion: 结果强有力支持24个代码质量指标的构念效度；耦合与继承是多维构念，复杂度可能更适合用多层模型探索，整体上展示了现代测量理论与潜变量建模在验证代码质量指标中的价值。

Abstract: Background: Code quality metrics are intended to measure latent properties of software source code. Although numerous code metrics have been proposed and used, their construct validity is rarely evaluated. Thus, the extent to which code metrics actually measure what they claim to measure is often unclear. Aim: Drawing from modern measurement theory, we investigate the construct validity of common class-level, object-oriented code quality metrics by identifying their factor structure using Exploratory Factor Analysis (EFA). The metrics were extracted from the Apache Maven project by three software tools: Designite, JHawk, and Understand. The factor structure was later verified using Confirmatory Factor Analysis (CFA) on 22 randomly selected open source projects meeting a predetermined eligibility criteria. Results: 24 code quality metrics that correspond to six constructs: Cohesion, In-Coupling, Out-Coupling, Size, Sub-Inheritance (related to subclasses), and Sup-Inheritance (related to superclasses) were revealed in the underlying factor structure. Ten metrics did not correspond to any known dimension of software quality and were removed in the EFA. Ten additional metrics exhibited low loadings in the CFA, suggesting their removal from the final measurement model. Size, Cohesion, Inheritance, and Coupling were the constructs retained, with subcategories identified for Inheritance and Coupling. Conclusions: Our results strongly support the construct validity of 24 code quality metrics. Coupling and Inheritance are revealed as multidimensional constructs, since they require measuring two different concepts, revealed as sub-categories in our analysis, and Complexity may be better explored in a multilevel model. Overall, our study demonstrates the value of applying modern measurement theory and latent variable modeling in validating software code quality metrics.

</details>


### [191] [Spotlights: Discovering Improvement Opportunities in Software Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20446)
*Udi Barzelay,Ophir Azulai,Idan Friedman,Inbar Shapira,Foad Abo Dahood,Yevgeny Burshtein,Orit Prince,Michael Soloveitchik,Oshri Naparstek,Roi Pony,Tal Drory,Michael Factor*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"优化机会发现"这一仓库级任务，并给出Spotlights系统，在无需用户指定缺陷或位置的前提下，发现值得研究的源码区域并提出改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理与进化式代码搜索系统只能在目标与评估标准已明确后改进实现；对于既有代码库，如何在高层工程目标下决定"哪些实现选择值得研究"这一更前置的问题尚未被形式化。

Method: 定义优化机会发现任务：输入为代码库、工程目标及可选的运行时证据（离线遥测、profile），输出候选源码区域、其与目标的关系解释及可能改动，无需用户指定缺陷、瓶颈或代码位置。Spotlights系统通过逻辑化代码库映射、连续代理审查，以及可选的研究关联（将候选与相关技术文献连接）来实现该任务。

Result: 在模型服务、文档检索、区块链排序和文档处理等场景评估：三个案例中命中专家选定的9个目标中的7个；可靠性研究中前十大候选70%满足正确性与严重性阈值；五次重复检索中73.6%的候选出现位置在五次运行中均有匹配源区域；重新发现了一个被隐藏的检索优化目标并关联到相关tiling技术；实现研究中一处发现的改动使端到端页面处理运行时降低10.6%，同时保持输出质量。

Conclusion: 优化机会发现是介于宽泛工程目标与后续实现/验证之间的一个独立且可实证评估的步骤。

Abstract: Coding agents and evolutionary code-search systems can improve implementations once a target and evaluation criterion have been specified. Applying these methods to an existing software repository raises an earlier question: which implementation choices are worth investigating for a high-level engineering objective? We introduce \emph{optimization-opportunity discovery}, the repository-level task of identifying candidate source regions, explaining how they relate to the objective, and proposing possible changes. The task takes as input a repository, an engineering objective, and optional runtime evidence such as offline telemetry observations or profiles. It does not require the user to specify a defect, bottleneck, or code location. We present \emph{Spotlights}, a system that performs this task through logical repository mapping, successive agent reviews, and optional research linking candidates to relevant techniques. We evaluate Spotlights across model serving, document retrieval, blockchain ordering, and document processing. Across three cases, it recovers seven of nine expert-selected targets. In the reliability study, 70\% of the top ten candidates meet the stated correctness and severity thresholds. Across five repeated retrieval runs, 73.6\% of candidate occurrences have a matching source region in all five runs. Spotlights also rediscovers the target of a withheld retrieval optimization and connects it to a relevant tiling technique. In an implementation study, a discovered change reduces end-to-end page-processing runtime by 10.6\% while preserving measured output quality. These results establish optimization-opportunity discovery as a distinct and empirically evaluable step between a broad engineering objective and subsequent implementation and validation.

</details>


### [192] [Quantifying Overclaiming Propensity in Frontier LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20812)
*Nolan Smyth,Yorguin-Jose Mantilla-Ramos,Pascal Jr Tikeng Notsawo,Saskia Helbling,Alberto Tosato,Mohamed Amine Merzouk,Nouha Dziri,Gauthier Gidel,Tommaso Tosato*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 OverclaimBench 评测套件，量化前沿编码智能体"过度声称"（overclaim）任务完成的倾向，发现智能体最终回复普遍不可靠地反映其真实行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿编码智能体被越来越多地信任可长时间自主工作，但其最终回复常常是用户了解其工作内容的唯一途径。若回复与上下文事实矛盾，可能误导用户，因此需要量化这种"过度声称"现象。

Method: 提出 overclaim 的无意图依赖定义（最终回复与上下文信息矛盾即算 overclaim），构建 OverclaimBench：含五个文件审查场景、基于对话记录的覆盖率度量及注册的植入缺陷。评测8个专有前沿模型（各自生产命令行界面）和4个开源权重模型（固定 harness）。

Result: 1) 67.9% 的 run 中智能体未读完全部要求审查的文件；2) 未读完的 run 中 80.4%（各模型 59–96%）存在误导，或谎称读全或隐瞒覆盖率不全；3) 要求委派子智能体可提高阅读覆盖率，但仍不完整的审查中大多数仍具误导性；4) 谎称完成审查的智能体漏掉植入缺陷的比率约为读全文件的智能体的 1.8 倍。

Conclusion: 智能体的最终回复并非其行为的可靠记录，声称完成可能掩盖实质失败，需对该类 overclaim 行为加以重视与治理。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents are increasingly trusted to work autonomously for long periods, yet an agent's final response is often the only account of that work a user sees. We quantify the propensity of frontier agents to \emph{overclaim} task completion, a misrepresentation that can mislead the user. An agent overclaims when its final response contradicts information in its context. This definition requires no inference about intent and is independent of task success. We introduce \emph{OverclaimBench}, an evaluation suite composed of five file-review scenarios, transcript-based coverage measurements, and registered planted defects. We evaluate eight proprietary frontier models in their own production command-line interfaces, and four open-weight models under a single fixed harness on OverclaimBench and find that 1) agents do not read all the files they were asked to review in 67.9\% of runs; 2) among runs where not all files are read, agents are \emph{misleading} 80.4\% of the time (59--96\% per model), either falsely claiming to have read all files or omitting that coverage is incomplete; 3) requiring delegation to subagents increased reading coverage, but among reviews that remained incomplete, a large majority were still misleading; and 4) agents that falsely claimed a complete review missed planted defects at about 1.8 times the rate of agents that read every file, showing that claims of completion can conceal substantive failures. Together, these results show that agents' final responses are not reliable accounts of their actions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning: Stability under Constant Stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19170)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Skylar Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究ETD在恒定步长采样动态下的稳定性，构造了两状态反例证明ETD均值映射收缩但采样乘积具正Lyapunov指数，并提出正则化ETD（RETD）修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ETD能稳定off-policy TD的期望更新并改变投影几何，但这两个性质都无法决定恒定步长下的采样动态稳定性，因此需要深入分析采样动态与几何稳定性的分离现象。

Method: 构造遍历两状态反例；用再生周期分析分离符号与follow-on trace无限方差；提出RETD——归一化一阶post-shock修复，不改trace和重要性比率，将emphatic TD信号存入漏泄标量状态并释放延迟校正。

Result: RETD原始均衡为ETD均衡的仿射偏移，单/双正则读出可精确恢复ETD不动点；证明调和递减步长的几乎必然收敛及条件恒定步长矩收缩；RETD在两状态构造和Baird点上获认证负指数，正Baird ETD符号仍为数值结果；10000次配对实验验证分离、不动点恢复、非单调稳定域与任务依赖性。

Conclusion: RETD改变了post-shock动态，但不降低共享follow-on-trace方差；期望几何稳定不等同于恒定步长采样稳定性。

Abstract: Emphatic temporal-difference learning (ETD) stabilizes the expected off-policy TD update and changes its projection geometry, but neither property determines constant-stepsize sampled dynamics. We construct an ergodic two-state counterexample in which the ETD mean map contracts while the sampled product has a positive top Lyapunov exponent. Regenerative-cycle analysis separates this sign from the infinite variance of the follow-on trace. We introduce regularized emphatic TD (RETD), a normalized first-order post-shock repair that leaves the trace and importance ratios unchanged, stores the emphatic TD signal in a leaky scalar state, and releases a delayed correction. RETD's raw equilibrium is an affine shift of the ETD equilibrium; single- and two-regularization readouts recover the ETD fixed point exactly. We prove almost-sure convergence for harmonic diminishing stepsizes and a conditional constant-stepsize moment-contraction result from a Markovian random-product bound. RETD has certified negative exponents on the two-state construction and one Baird point, whereas the positive Baird ETD sign remains numerical. Paired 10,000-run experiments validate both separations, fixed-point recovery, a nonmonotone stability region, and task dependence. RETD changes post-shock dynamics; it does not reduce the shared follow-on-trace variance.

</details>


### [194] [BioPhys-Bridge: A Benchmark for Interdisciplinary Scientific Reasoning in Physics-Grounded Biological Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19180)
*Qingyang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出BioPhys-Bridge，一个面向生物物理文献的循证科学推理基准数据集，包含500个案例和1,517个任务，评估语言模型在跨学科科学推理中的归因、忠实度与幻觉减少能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在分析跨学科科研文献时面临挑战：生物物理研究中，忠实回答需将观测数据根植于源证据、通过定量物理模型解读、并链接到生物机制，现有基准难以覆盖这种复杂的多步科学推理需求。

Method: 构建BioPhys-Bridge基准数据集：每个案例包含证据块、稳定的证据ID、定量数值、单位、方程、假设、机制及下一步决策等作为问答（QA）和检索增强生成（RAG）的锚定目标。初版含500个案例、1,517个面向智能体的任务，覆盖6个生物学领域和9个物理模型族（含3个稀疏族留待扩展）。设置严格质量门槛（模式、证据完整性、定量锚定、源许可、去重、单位归一化），并对81个案例进行领域专家审查与标注。

Result: 初步评估显示，DeepSeek-V4-Flash取得最高的证据ID F₁分数（0.360），其后为Qwen3.7-Max（0.316）和GPT-4o-mini（0.294）。

Conclusion: BioPhys-Bridge是一个跨学科基准，用于评估复杂多步科学推理中的归因、忠实度、幻觉减少与生物实验设计能力。未来工作将扩大数据集规模与复杂度，并进行全面评估。代码与数据已在GitHub和Hugging Face上公开。

Abstract: Language models face unique challenges in analyzing interdisciplinary scientific research literature. In biophysics research, faithful answers require grounding observed data in source evidence, interpreting it through a quantitative physics model, and linking it to a biological mechanism. To address this challenge, we introduce BioPhys-Bridge, a novel benchmark dataset for evidence-grounded scientific reasoning over biophysical literature. Each case contains evidence blocks, stable evidence IDs, quantitative values, units, equations, assumptions, mechanisms, and next decisions as grounding targets for question answering (QA) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The initial release contains 500 cases, 1,517 agent-facing tasks, and covers six biological domains and nine physical model families, including three sparse families reserved for future expansion. We enforce strict quality gates for all cases in schema, evidence-integrity, quantitative-grounding, source-license, duplicate, unit-normalization, with domain expert review and annotation for 81 cases. Preliminary evaluations show that DeepSeek-V4-Flash obtain the highest evidence-ID $F_1$ score (0.360), followed by Qwen3.7-Max (0.316) and GPT-4o-mini (0.294). BioPhys-Bridge is an interdisciplinary benchmark for evaluating attribution, faithfulness, hallucination reduction, and biological experiment design with complex, multi-step scientific reasoning. Future works will increase the size and complexity of the dataset and perform comprehensive evaluations. Code and data are available in the GitHub repository and on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [195] [What Do We Expect from LLMs? Mapping the Design of LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19182)
*Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理2022-2026年间arXiv上14,767篇提出或更新评测资源的论文，通过分阶段筛选与自动化全文编码，追踪LLM评测在目标系统、领域、材料条件和评分机制上的演变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型排行榜本身难以揭示评测需求如何变化。作者认为，不断增多的基准测试提供了另一视角：研究者期望LLM做什么、以及把什么视为成功表现。同时随着AI参与到出题、执行任务和评判回答中，评测的独立性问题值得关注。

Method: 对2022年1月至2026年8月arXiv提交的14,767篇引入或更新评测资源的论文进行系统映射，采用分阶段筛选（staged screening）与自动化全文编码（automated full-text coding），考察目标系统与领域、评测材料与条件、评分机制的变化。

Result: 评测日益强调行动（action）、交互（interaction）和专业应用（professional applications），新旧设计元素频繁共存。模型参与度发展不均衡：基于LLM的评分在agent与非agent组中均增长，而模型生成的材料在近期批次中未出现可持续增长。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了公共研究如何把能力期待转化为具体的测试与成功标准。随着AI参与构建测试、执行任务和评判回答，也提出一个关键问题：评测的扩张带来的是更多独立证据，还是可能复制参与模型的偏好与盲区。

Abstract: Benchmarks are central to how progress in large language models (LLMs) is assessed and communicated. Yet model rankings alone reveal little about how evaluation requirements themselves are changing. The expanding variety of benchmarks offers another perspective: what researchers expect LLMs to do, and what they count as successful performance. We systematically map 14,767 papers introducing or updating evaluation resources from arXiv submissions between January 2022 and August 2026. Using staged screening and automated full-text coding, we examine changes in target systems and domains, evaluation materials and conditions, and scoring mechanisms. The collection shows growing emphasis on action, interaction, and professional applications, while established and newer design elements frequently coexist. Model participation also develops unevenly: LLM-based scoring grows within both agent and non-agent groups, whereas model-generated materials show no comparable sustained increase in recent cohorts. These findings illuminate how public research translates capability expectations into concrete tests and criteria for success. As AI participates in constructing tests, performing tasks, and judging responses, they also raise a question: does expanding evaluation provide more independent evidence, or risk reproducing the preferences and blind spots of its participating models?

</details>


### [196] [Position: It is Time to Virtualize Foundation Models with a Self-evolving Operating System Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19203)
*Suparna Bhattacharya,Tarun Kumar,Cong Xu,Satish Kumar Mopur,Jiahao Li,Ashish Mishra,Aalap Tripathy,Annmary Justine Koomthanam,Martin Foltin,Ian Foster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张AI领域需要构建"基础模型操作系统(FMOS)"，作为虚拟化基础模型交互的系统层，为应用提供专有、可信、近乎无限能力的基础模型实例抽象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI应用已从单一单体基础模型转向复合智能体系统，但当前技术栈高度碎片化：虽然MCP、A2A等协议简化了工具/智能体连接，各框架仍内嵌隐式的状态、记忆、预算和护栏运行时机制，导致行为不可移植、治理脆弱。这如同操作系统出现之前的计算时代，每个程序都重复实现基础服务。

Method: 提出FMOS系统层概念：内部编排跨记忆层级的知识、模型选择与资源分配，以及验证与策略执行；借鉴人脑在快速直觉与慢速深思之间的切换机制，FMOS学习何时介入、何时让推理直接推进，并基于运行经验持续调整策略。

Result: 作为立场论文，本文提出概念性框架与架构愿景，未报告实验性结果，而是论证FMOS作为虚拟化FM交互系统层的必要性及其核心组成。

Conclusion: AI领域需要FMOS作为系统层来统一和虚拟化基础模型交互，解决当前碎片化、不可移植和治理脆弱的问题，实现行为可移植、治理稳健的复合智能体系统。

Abstract: AI applications have shifted from single, monolithic foundation models (FM) to compound agentic systems. Yet today's stacks remain fragmented: even as protocols (e.g., MCP, A2A) ease tool/agent connectivity, each framework embeds an implicit runtime for state, memory, budgets, and guardrails, making behavior non-portable and governance brittle. It mirrors computing before operating systems, when every program re-implemented basic services. This position paper argues that the field now needs a Foundation Model Operating System (FMOS) -- a system layer that virtualizes FM interactions analogous to how virtual machines abstract physical hardware, giving applications the illusion of dedicated, trustworthy FM instances with effectively unbounded capabilities. Internally, the FMOS orchestrates knowledge across memory tiers, model selection and resource allocation, and verification and policy enforcement. Like the human brain switching between fast intuition and slow deliberation, the FMOS learns when to intervene and when to let inference proceed directly and continuously adapting its policies based on operational experience.

</details>


### [197] [What Do Current Systematic Generalization Tasks Miss? A Reasoning-Centered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19212)
*Chengwen Qi,Deheng Ye,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统泛化难以严格研究，现有任务通过简化（近线性动作组合、基于生产力的测试、动作显式目标）忽略了演绎、归纳和溯因推理。作者提出TranSGrid测试平台统一三类推理，七个Transformer在4,800个实例上表现大幅下降（最大模型测试集79.6% vs. TranSGrid 55.3%，最难子集15.8%），证明生产力不足以评估系统泛化，而重新引入任一简化都会使性能回到测试集水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统泛化是人类智能的核心，但现有研究依赖简化假设（近线性动作组合、生产力测试、动作显式目标），这些简化掩盖了该能力的本质方面。作者希望刻画这些简化究竟遗漏了什么，从而更全面地评估系统泛化。

Method: 以推理为中心，提出TranSGrid测试平台，将演绎、归纳和溯因三种推理统一在同一任务中。在4,800个TranSGrid实例上测试七个Transformer；并设计两个变体，分别重新引入近线性动作组合（降低归纳需求）和动作显式目标（降低溯因需求），进行消融对比。

Result: 所有模型在TranSGrid上表现远差于留出测试集：最大模型测试集解出79.6%，但TranSGrid仅55.3%，最难子集仅15.8%，且差距在训练长度范围内依然存在。重新引入任一简化后，解出率均回升至接近测试集水平。

Conclusion: 现有任务降低了归纳和/或溯因需求；生产力单独不足以评估系统泛化。全面衡量系统泛化需要同时涉及演绎、归纳和溯因三种推理的任务。

Abstract: Systematic generalization, the ability to solve novel problems by recombining known atomic elements, is central to human intelligence but difficult to study rigorously under controlled settings. Existing studies therefore rely on simplifications such as approximately linear action composition, productivity-based tests, and action-explicit goals, which make systematic generalization easier to study but omit some essential aspects of this capability. To characterize what these simplifications miss, we adopt a reasoning-centered lens and introduce TranSGrid, a testbed that brings deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning together within a unified task. Experiments with seven Transformers on 4,800 TranSGrid instances show that all models perform much worse on TranSGrid than on a held-out test set: the largest model solves 79.6% of the test set, but only 55.3% of TranSGrid and 15.8% of the hardest subset. The gap remains within the training length range, showing that productivity alone is not sufficient to evaluate systematic generalization. Additionally, we reintroduce the other two simplifications into TranSGrid: one variant makes actions compose almost linearly (reducing the inductive demand), the other makes goals action-explicit (reducing the abductive one). In both, solve rates return to roughly the test set level, showing that either simplification alone is enough to reduce TranSGrid to an ordinary held-out test set. Together, our results show that existing tasks reduce either or both of the inductive and abductive demands, and that comprehensively measuring systematic generalization requires a task that involves all three forms of reasoning.

</details>


### [198] [AI-Driven Real-Time Relay Optimisation in Smart Urban NR-V2X Networks via Learning-to-Optimise Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20271)
*Giambattista Amati,Federica Mangiatordi,Emiliano Pallotti,Simone Angelini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图神经网络(GNN)的AI驱动学习优化(L2O)框架，用于NR-V2X系统中的实时多跳中继选择，以接近最优的性能和高达100倍的加速实现可靠低时延通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能城市服务与工业4.0应用需要NR-V2X网络提供可靠低时延通信。但RSU部署有限且城市传播条件复杂，导致网联自动驾驶车辆(CAV)难以维持稳定连接。

Method: 将车联网建模为图（节点为CAV和RSU，边编码无线链路特征），用离线MILP求解最优中继决策作为监督信号，训练边感知的带边特征图同构网络(GINE)进行实时多跳中继选择。

Result: 在真实城市数据集上，方法达到接近最优的连通性，最多获得11.3%连通性增益，同时相比MILP执行时间降低数个数量级（最高100倍加速）。

Conclusion: 该框架支持可扩展的实时网络控制，适用于智能城市和工业4.0部署。

Abstract: Reliable and low-latency communication is a fundamental requirement for smart city services and Industry 4.0 applications enabled by NR-V2X networks. However, limited Road-Side Unit (RSU) deployment and complex urban propagation conditions often prevent Connected and Automated Vehicles (CAVs) from maintaining stable connectivity. This paper proposes an AI-driven Learning-to-Optimise (L2O) framework based on Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) for real-time multi-hop relay selection in NR-V2X systems. The vehicular network is modelled as a graph, where nodes represent CAVs and RSUs, and edges encode radio-link characteristics. An offline Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) formulation provides optimal relay decisions used as supervision for training an edge-aware Graph Isomorphism Network with Edge Features (GINE). Extensive experiments on realistic urban datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves near-optimal connectivity performance, recovering up to 11.3% connectivity gain, while reducing execution time by orders of magnitude (up to 100 x speed-up) compared to MILP. The framework enables scalable and real-time network control, making it suitable for smart city and Industry 4.0 deployments.

</details>


### [199] [Characterizing Web Search by Conversational LLM Agents: From Search Decisions and Strategies to Results and Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19244)
*Mahsa Amani,Seungeon Lee,Abhisek Dash,Asmaa El Fraihi,Yunah Jang,Elisabeth Kirsten,Qinyuan Wu,Krishna P. Gummadi,Manish Gupta,Abhilasha Ravichander,Muhammad Bilal Zafar,Soumi Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个研究跨四大对话平台（ChatGPT、Claude、Grok、DeepSeek）的智能体式网页搜索全生命周期，揭示搜索决策、查询策略、域名偏好及结果落地行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式LLM智能体日益依赖网页搜索，但其端到端搜索生命周期仍缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 结合真实用户交互（invivo）与通过API复现同平台模型的受控实验（invitro），研究四平台的搜索调用决策、查询构建策略、结果域名偏好及结果转化行为。

Result: 搜索决策跨平台/模型差异显著，更频繁调用搜索未必带来更优质回答；智能体采用不同的复杂查询策略；各平台搜索引擎倾向返回偏好域名的结果；回答虽大体有据，但部分论断依赖未被引用的搜索结果。

Conclusion: 研究对未来AI智能体与面向对话检索优化的网页搜索工具设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Conversational LLM agents increasingly rely on Web search, yet the end-to-end lifecycle of agentic search remains poorly understood. We present the first study of Web search across four major conversational platforms (ChatGPT, Claude, Grok, and DeepSeek), combining real-world user interactions (invivo) with controlled experiments using the same platform's models by their APIs (invitro). We investigate the quality of agentic decisions to invoke Web search, their strategies to formulate queries, the potential domain preferences in the search results they receive, and the choices they make when transforming search results into grounded responses. We find that Web-search decisions vary substantially across platforms and models, while more frequent Web-search invocation does not necessarily yield better response quality. We further show that conversational agents employ different complex querying strategies and that platform specific search engines return search results from their preferred domains. Finally, although responses are largely grounded in search results, some claims rely on uncited search results, raising concerns about attribution and reliability. Our findings have important implications for the design of future AI agents and Web search tools optimized for conversational retrieval.

</details>


### [200] [Do AI Agents Understand Computer Architecture?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19387)
*Ambika Sharan,Grigory Chirkov,Soheil Abbasloo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoTuring通过让同一智能体在"有语义的架构旋钮"与"无语义的匿名变量"两种表述下求解同一加速器设计问题，测量"意义"本身带来的增益，并以受控对比（而非加速器成果）作为核心贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测只改变智能体而固定问题表述，无法区分智能体是"真正理解机器"还是"在无意义旋钮上盲目搜索"，而只有前者才能迁移到下一个架构。因此需要一种能分离"理解"与"搜索能力"的测量方法。

Method: 给同一智能体同一15维加速器空间两次：一次作为带模拟器计数器的命名架构旋钮，一次作为[0,1]上的匿名变量；评估器、合法空间、可达最优点完全一致，唯一变量是问题是否有意义。两者的差距即为"意义"的度量。在九核FP16 GEMM任务上比较architect与blind两种条件，并加入critic循环检验互补性。

Result: 在九核FP16 GEMM任务上，architect比建模的H200平均快5.4%，比blind对手平均快12.3%，且模拟器调用少70.1%。意义并非不可替代：critic循环可为blind智能体追回大部分差距，却对architect毫无增益——说明架构知识与结构化批判是替代关系而非互补关系。

Conclusion: 结论为初步发现（每条件5-6次运行，单一建模加速器）；作者强调真正贡献是这一对比方法本身，而非加速器性能提升。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly asked to design hardware, and increasingly reported to succeed. Such reports establish that a design improved; they cannot establish why. An agent that improves an accelerator may be reasoning about the machine, or may be searching competently over knobs whose meaning it never recovers -- and only the first transfers to the next architecture. Existing evaluations cannot tell the two apart, because they vary the agent while holding the framing of the problem fixed. We do the opposite. AutoTuring hands the same agent the same 15-dimensional accelerator space twice: once as named architectural knobs with simulator counters, once as anonymous variables on [0,1], with the evaluator, the legal space and the reachable optima held identical, so that the only thing that varies is whether the problem means anything. The gap between the two is the measurement. On a nine-kernel FP16 GEMM basket, meaning pays: the architect beats a modeled H200 by 5.4% and its blind counterpart by 12.3% on average, with 70.1% fewer simulator calls. It does not pay uniquely: a critic loop recovers most of that gap for the blind agent and buys the architect nothing, so architectural knowledge and structured critique behave as substitutes rather than as complements. We report these as preliminary findings -- five to six runs per condition on a single modeled accelerator -- and take the comparison itself, not the accelerator, to be the contribution.

</details>


### [201] [MAGS: Multi-agent Auto-formalization Guarantees Safety for Agentic Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19391)
*Albert Wu,Nicholas Roberts,Tzu-Heng Huang,Haoran Lin,Gil Friedman,Sungjun Cho,Gabriel Orlanski,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGS是一个多智能体框架，用Dafny作为验证中间表示，为LLM生成程序提供形式化安全保证，在220个任务上实现100%通过冻结规范的验证成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体大规模生成复杂程序，人工审查困难，存在安全风险；模糊测试、静态分析、LLM验证器难覆盖所有边界情况，而形式化验证虽能提供机器可检查保证，但传统上需要大量人工规范和证明工程。

Method: 提出MAGS统一多智能体框架：形式化并冻结人工审计的API与安全需求，将生成代码翻译为Dafny，利用验证器反馈修复违规，再将验证通过的程序编译回可执行代码。

Result: 在100个CUDA内核、100个终端脚本、20个机械臂任务（共220例）上，MAGS对冻结规范实现了100%生成含非平凡安全保证程序的成功率；独立安全与功能评估在三领域表现强劲，但在自动形式化语义未能完全捕捉目标行为时出现失败。

Conclusion: MAGS证明多智能体+验证感知IR可在无需大量人工证明工程的前提下，为LLM生成程序提供可扩展的形式化安全保证，但自动形式化语义的准确性是主要局限。

Abstract: LLM coding agents now generate complex programs at a scale that makes thorough human review increasingly difficult, raising the risk of safety and security failures. Common approaches, including fuzz testing, static analysis, and LLM-as-a-Verifier, can detect many failures but struggle to cover all possible edge cases. Formal verification addresses this by providing machine-checkable guarantees over specified properties, but traditionally demands substantial manual specification and proof engineering. We introduce a unified multi-agent framework, MAGS, that generates executable programs with formal safety guarantees, using Dafny as a verification-aware intermediate representation where safety properties can be mechanically checked. MAGS formalizes and freezes human-audited APIs and safety requirements, translates generated code into Dafny, repairs violations using verifier feedback, and compiles verified programs back into executable code. We evaluate MAGS on 100 CUDA kernels, 100 terminal scripts, and 20 robotic-arm tasks. Across all 220 examples, it achieves a 100% success rate in producing programs with non-trivial safety guarantees against frozen specifications. Independent safety and functional evaluations further show strong performance across all three domains, while revealing failures when the auto-formalized semantics do not fully capture the target behavior.

</details>


### [202] [Closed-World Resolution Against Tool Hallucination in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19425)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是测量与基准研究，揭示工具增强LLM智能体的"工具幻觉"（调用不存在的工具）是现有选择/门控方法的结构性盲区，并提出分类学、证明防御须先于因果门控，发布HTB基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御聚焦工具选择或安全门控，但都预设调用指向真实工具。幻觉调用在构造上不经任何门控决策，故无门可拒，这是被忽视的结构性盲区。

Method: 提出五类工具幻觉分类（H1-H5）与无训练、封闭世界的Resolution Rung解析器（注册表成员检查+签名校验）；扩展至MCP，提出第二分类（M1-M5）；在十款托管模型、两种调用面上测量。

Result: 测得322次真实幻觉，伪造工具调用集中于无约束raw-JSON面（34 vs. 3）；模型规模无帮助（675B与7-8B相当）；实时MCP面测得154次幻觉，含在单注册表面干净的前沿模型。

Conclusion: 证明幻觉防御必须先于因果门控；刻画不可约残余（借用参数与有效调用签名无法区分）；MCP合并命名空间造成单注册表无法表达的结构性冲突与遮蔽；发布版本化HTB基准供解析器跨提交比较。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model (LLM) agents fail in a way no tool-selection or tool-security method addresses: they call tools that do not exist and pass arguments no schema declares. Existing defenses either pick the right tool (selection) or constrain what an agent may do with real tools (gating), both of which presuppose the emitted call refers to a real tool at all. We show this is a structural blind spot: a hallucinated call is by construction not a decision any gate made, so no gate can reject it. This paper is primarily a measurement and benchmark study. We give a five-class taxonomy of tool hallucination (H1-H5) and, as a reference point, the Resolution Rung: a training-free, closed-world resolver (registry membership plus a signature check) whose interest is where it must sit, not what it computes. We prove hallucination defense must precede any causal gate, and characterize the one irreducible residue (borrowed arguments schema-indistinguishable from a valid call). Across ten hosted models under two invocation surfaces we measure 322 genuine hallucinations; fabricated-tool calls concentrate on the unconstrained raw-JSON surface (34 vs. 3), and model scale does not help (a 675B model matches a 7-8B one). We then extend to the Model Context Protocol, where merging several servers into one namespace creates hallucination surfaces a single registry cannot express (a second taxonomy, M1-M5); on the live MCP surface we measure 154 hallucinations, including from frontier models that were clean on the single-registry surface, because collisions and shadowing are structural to the merge. We release the versioned Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark (HTB) so any resolver is comparable across submissions.

</details>


### [203] [The syntax and semantics of goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19448)
*David M. Abel,Mark K. Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将"目标"视为可组合的表征，其内容与理性行为相关，并主张从表征的"形式与意义"角度刻画不同目标表征的性质，将其置于更广泛的设计空间中考察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学与计算机科学中，目标通常被当作固定、施加约束的认知状态，但其表征本身的表达力、设计与效率等基础问题较少被系统关注。作者借助语言学与逻辑学中"句法-语义接口"的类比，提出应当审视目标表征自身的性质。

Method: 概念性与综述性分析：将目标理解为可组合表征，强调其内容与理性行为的联系，并综合已有工作来刻画不同目标表征的属性，将其视为一个更大设计空间的若干要点。

Result: 识别出关于目标表征的一系列基础问题，例如某种目标语言是否足够表达所关注的行为、不同目标表征是否刻画同一行为，并提出这些构成一个统一的设计空间。

Conclusion: 区分目标的"形式"与"意义"有助于澄清我们对目标所作的隐含假设，促进对高层认知与动机交互的研究，并为不同目标概念分离出变异维度。

Abstract: In both cognitive science and computer science, goals are conceptualized as cognitive states that flexibly combine with world knowledge to organize and specify purposeful behavior. In this way, goals are compositional representations whose content relates to rational behavior. We here draw attention to goals as representations and their content because it highlights a parallel with other areas in cognitive science - in particular, the syntax-semantics interface in linguistics and logic - while also foregrounding foundational questions about the expressivity, design, and efficiency of different goal representations. For example, goals are typically taken as fixed and imposing constraints on desirable behaviors, but we can also identify constraints on goal representations themselves, such as whether a particular goal language is sufficiently expressive to capture behaviors of interest, or whether different goal representations capture the same behavior. Here, we synthesize work that aims to characterize the properties of different goal representations and suggest these are points of a broader design space. We close by discussing how distinguishing the form and meaning of goals can elucidate the implicit assumptions we make about goals, inform the study of interactions between higher-level cognition and motivation, and isolate axes of variation for different conceptions of goals.

</details>


### [204] [Compositional Reasoning in Language Models under Reinforcement Learning Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19465)
*Yu He,Yingxi Li,Yifei Wang,Ellen Vitercik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出依赖图框架形式化组合推理，定义三层复杂度，用数据结构任务实证发现"分解→组合"的不对称性：分解技能训练难以迁移到组合任务，而组合任务训练更易迁移回分解任务，并给出理论解释与多场景评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据有限，模型必须通过组合已学技能来泛化到新问题。尽管RL等后训练方法大幅提升了LM推理能力，但对其组合推理能力的影响仍不清楚，需要一个能形式化组合推理的框架来系统研究这一问题。

Method: 提出依赖图（dependency-graph）框架来形式化组合推理，给出三层递增复杂度的组合性定义。以数据结构任务为实例（奖励确定性、组合结构清晰）进行实证研究，并进一步在长度外推、结构分布偏移、需要未见技能的任务迁移下评估组合泛化。

Result: 发现一致的"分解到组合"不对称性：分解技能训练不能可靠迁移到组合任务，而组合任务训练更易迁移回分解任务；提供了理论解释；并在真实工具调用基准的初步研究中发现该不对称性可能延伸至实际场景。

Conclusion: 组合推理的训练迁移存在方向性不对称，框架与理论解释为理解LM组合泛化提供新视角，且该现象在实际任务中初现端倪。

Abstract: Compositional reasoning is critical for real-world problem solving: since training data is necessarily limited, models must generalize by composing learned skills in new ways. While post-training methods such as reinforcement learning (RL) have substantially improved the reasoning abilities of language models (LMs), their effects on compositional reasoning remain less well understood. We propose a dependency-graph framework to formalize compositional reasoning, yielding three levels of compositionality with increasing complexity. Empirically, we instantiate this framework with data-structure tasks, which provide deterministic reward computation and clear compositional structure. We find a consistent decomposed-to-composed asymmetry: decomposed-skill training does not reliably transfer to composed tasks, whereas composed-task training transfers more readily back to decomposed tasks. We provide theoretical explanation for this asymmetry, and further evaluate compositional generalization under length extrapolation, structural distribution shift, and transfer to tasks requiring unseen skills. Finally, we present a pilot study on real-world tool-calling benchmarks, showing preliminary evidence that the decomposed-to-composed asymmetry can extend to practical settings.

</details>


### [205] [Safety Beyond the Interface: Detecting Harm via Latent States in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19472)
*Alizishaan Khatri,Chiquita Prabhu,Omkar Neogi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过提取LLaMA-3.1-8B内部激活训练轻量级MLP探针(12.6M参数)检测有害提示，性能媲美千倍大的守卫模型，同时降低延迟与算力开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统依赖LLM但安全基础设施带来延迟与算力开销，资源受限场景受限。现有外部守卫模型无法洞察模型内部，存在保障缺口。作者提出疑问：模型内部是否已知内容有害？

Method: 从LLaMA-3.1-8B提取激活，训练轻量级MLP分类探针(约12.6M参数)，用于检测有害提示。

Result: 在WildJailbreak、Beavertails、AEGIS 2.0上F1分别达99%、83%、84%，与千倍大的守卫模型相当。

Conclusion: 利用模型内部激活的探针可高效检测有害内容，在保持竞争力的同时降低延迟与算力成本。

Abstract: Autonomous systems increasingly rely on Large Language Models (LLMs) yet the safety infrastructure surrounding these models introduces latency and compute overhead. This limits utility in resource-constrained, time-critical deployments. Existing external guardrail models remain blind to the model's internal workings, creating a fundamental assurance gap. We ask: does the model already know when the content is harmful? We extract activations from LLaMA-3.1-8B and train lightweight MLP classifier probes (12.6M parameters) to detect harmful prompts. Evaluated on WildJailbreak, Beavertails, and AEGIS 2.0, our probes achieve F1 scores of 99%, 83%, and 84%, respectively competitive with 1000x larger guard models while cutting latency and compute costs.

</details>


### [206] [QVAC Genesis III: A Large-Scale, High-Quality Open Synthetic STEM Corpus for Efficient Language Model Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19513)
*Davide Vitabile,N. Ranjan,Akshay Nambiar,Kamal K. Gupta,Amril Nazir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QVAC Genesis III，一个191.43B token、覆盖19个STEM领域的多域合成语料，通过双生成策略（利用边缘小模型失败/成功信号做教师蒸馏）构建，并以LLM-as-a-parser协议评估。1.7B参数从头训练实验表明其优于Cosmopedia-v2和Cosmo-1B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量预训练数据是面向边缘AI/端侧部署、token预算紧张的STEM教育语言模型的关键瓶颈；开源生态缺乏高"每token学习价值"、适合小模型的STEM合成数据集，而大机构多用私有语料训练超大模型。

Method: 构建QVAC Genesis III：191.43B token、19个STEM领域、多难度层级与多种教育风格的合成语料。采用双生成策略做针对性教师蒸馏，以弱边缘规模学生模型为信号：将其失败转化为纠正性解释，将其成功扩展为覆盖所有选项的对比式选项级推理。同时提出LLM-as-a-parser评估协议，从自由文本中抽取最终答案，追踪准确率与答案有效率。

Result: 以1.7B参数模型从头训练进行受控消融：使用QVAC Genesis III的模型在ARC、GPQA Diamond、MMLU STEM上一致优于Cosmopedia-v2训练模型及公开的Cosmo-1B；ARC-E最高提升+28.57%，ARC-C最高+21.35%，有效答案率达99.45%。

Conclusion: QVAC Genesis III证明以弱学生模型失败/成功为信号的双生成教师蒸馏可产出高学习价值的STEM合成语料，在受限token预算下显著提升小模型STEM能力，为端侧教育模型提供可行数据方案。

Abstract: High-quality pre-training data is a critical bottleneck for educational and STEM-specific language models targeting edge AI and on-device deployment where token budgets are tightly constrained. While major organizations train ever-larger models on private corpora, the open ecosystem lacks STEM-focused synthetic datasets that deliver high per-token learning value efficiently for small models. To address this gap, we introduce QVAC Genesis III, a 191.43B-token, STEM-focused multi-domain synthetic corpus covering 19 domains across several difficulty levels and different educational styles. QVAC Genesis III is built via a dual generation strategy that performs targeted teacher distillation using a weak edge-scale student model as signal: the student's failures are converted into corrective explanations, while its successes are expanded into contrastive option-level reasoning over all answer choices. We further introduce an LLM-as-a-parser evaluation protocol that extracts final answers from free-form outputs and tracks both accuracy and answer validity. To validate the effectiveness of our QVAC Genesis III data, we conduct controlled from-scratch ablations with 1.7B-parameter models, showing that models trained with QVAC Genesis III consistently outperform both models trained with the open-source synthetic corpus Cosmopedia-v2 and the publicly released Cosmo-1B model across ARC, GPQA Diamond, and MMLU STEM benchmarks, achieving up to +28.57% on ARC-E and +21.35% on ARC-C, while reaching a Valid Answer Rate of up to 99.45%.

</details>


### [207] [LLM-as-an-Improver: Turning Verification into Better Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19515)
*Akiyoshi Tomihari,Yuma Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LLM-as-an-Improver 与 Verify–Repair–Reselect（VRR）框架，利用验证反馈不仅做候选排序，还生成并重选更优候选，从而超越固定候选池的选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于验证器的选择方法把验证仅当作排序步骤，一旦在固定候选池上评估完毕就丢弃验证反馈，无法改善候选集本身。作者希望探究验证反馈能否用于构造更强的候选解。

Method: 提出 VRR：先用验证反馈保留初始最优解，并条件性地生成三种互补候选（最优解的修复版、次优解的修复版、基于新思路的解）；仅利用推理时信息过滤无效和重复候选，最后在原评估标准下重选最终答案。

Result: 在多种模型以及代码生成与推理基准上，VRR 在诸多设置中优于固定池的验证器选择方法，甚至能在初始池全部错误时恢复正确解。

Conclusion: 验证反馈的作用可扩展：LLM 不仅是选择者，还可作为改进者，利用反馈构建超越初始候选池的更强候选，拓展了验证器在 LLM 推理中的作用。

Abstract: Verifier-based selection improves LLM performance by generating multiple candidate solutions and using a verifier to select the most promising one. However, existing methods typically treat verification only as a ranking step and discard its feedback once a fixed candidate pool has been evaluated. In this paper, we ask whether verification can also improve the candidate set itself. To this end, we introduce LLM-as-an-Improver and propose Verify--Repair--Reselect (VRR), which uses verification feedback to generate and reselect improved candidates. VRR retains the initial winner while conditionally generating three complementary alternatives: repaired versions of the winner and runner-up, and a solution based on a new approach. It filters invalid and duplicate candidates using only inference-time information and then reselects the final answer under the original evaluation criteria. Across diverse models and code-generation and reasoning benchmarks, VRR improves over fixed-pool verifier-based selection in many settings and can recover correct solutions even when all candidates in the initial pool are incorrect. These results highlight a broader role for LLMs as improvers: verification feedback can not only select among existing solutions but also construct stronger candidates beyond the initial pool.

</details>


### [208] [An Architecture for Long-Horizon Agents: Levels, Ticks and Cascaded Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19519)
*Erik Nijkamp,Anurag Koul,Egor Pakhomov,Bo Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 长时程智能体需先做到"持续运行且不遗忘"，能力在于模型外的harness；提出分层架构（时间尺度分层+时钟tick+级联智能），经十天实验验证跨会话不遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务（如运维修复、研究项目）跨越数天乃至数周，超出任何上下文窗口、进程与人工关注间隔，现有智能体难以在持续运行中保持记忆与学习。

Method: 提出七项长时程瓶颈，并用三层架构应对：(i) 按时间尺度分层的层级，每层用有界文件总结下层；(ii) 以"时钟tick"作为自主行动单元；(iii) 级联智能，仅在审核失败后将任务升级给更强模型。

Result: 开展为期十天、人每天仅关注一次的实验，智能体复现了已发表的强化学习结果：跨所有上下文重置与会话边界保持任务线索；早期写入的操作知识在不改变模型权重的情况下改变后续行为；并给出了学习组件可嵌入的位置。

Conclusion: 此类智能体的持续学习需要一种超越所有上下文与进程的基底，而harness中已有的校验环节正是学习器应存在之处。

Abstract: Language-model agents are increasingly asked to carry out work spanning days or weeks, such as an operations remediation or a research programme. Such a task outlives any context window, any process and any interval at which a person can attend. In this paper, we argue that a long-horizon agent must run continually without forgetting before it can learn continually. This ability lies in the harness around the model rather than in the model itself. We derive seven bottlenecks from the long-horizon setting and answer them with a hierarchical architecture of three parts: (i) levels indexed by time scale, each keeping a bounded file summarising the level below; (ii) a clocked tick as the unit of autonomous action; and (iii) cascaded intelligence, where work is escalated to a more capable model only after failing review. We report on a ten-day campaign in which an agent built on this architecture reproduced a published reinforcement-learning result with a human attending once a day, and show (1) the agent kept the thread across every context reset and session boundary of the campaign, (2) operating knowledge written early changed later behaviour with no change to model weights, and (3) where learned components would enter such a system. Overall, our experience suggests continual learning for these agents needs a substrate outliving every context and process, and the checks the harness already runs are where a learner belongs.

</details>


### [209] [EconSkills: Studying Skill Transfer and Retrieval for Web Agents on Live Economic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19523)
*Yinzhu Quan,Zefang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 EconSkills，将已验证的 EconWebArena 轨迹蒸馏为参数化标准操作流程（SOP）技能库，评估技能在任务间迁移及大规模库检索中的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web agent 经常重访相同站点，但多数评估丢弃了先前成功交互中习得的流程，导致经验无法复用。

Method: 从已验证轨迹中提取技能，每个技能记录范围、导航过程、站点指导、验证检查与恢复步骤，并将具体源实例值替换为占位符；区分两个问题：已知相关流程是否迁移到留出任务，以及从库中选择时能否保留收益。

Result: 受控迁移中，匹配技能提升成功率并在配对成功上减少步数，抽象化优于重放原始轨迹；库规模下检索整体与无技能基线相当，在直接覆盖任务上最佳，但未覆盖任务的近似匹配抵消部分增益；浏览器轨迹揭示程序指导何时缩短特定门户导航、何时仍需语义验证。

Conclusion: 可复用的经济类 Web 流程能跨任务实例迁移，为覆盖感知的选择与上下文传递提供了具体设计目标。

Abstract: Web agents often revisit the same sites, yet most evaluations discard the procedures learned in earlier successful interactions. We introduce EconSkills, a skill library and evaluation framework that distills verified EconWebArena trajectories into parameterized standard operating procedures for retrieving live economic data. Each skill records its scope, navigation procedure, site-specific guidance, verification checks, and recovery steps while replacing source-instance values with placeholders. EconSkills separates two questions: whether a known relevant procedure transfers to a held-out task, and whether an agent can retain that benefit when selecting from a library. In controlled transfer, matched skills improve success over no-skill prompting and require fewer steps on paired successes, while abstraction is substantially more effective than replaying raw trajectories. At library scale, retrieval is competitive with the no-skill baseline overall and performs best on directly covered tasks; coverage-stratified outcomes show that approximate matches on uncovered tasks offset these gains. Browser trajectories further identify when procedural guidance shortens portal-specific navigation and when semantic verification remains necessary. These results establish that reusable economic web procedures can transfer across task instances and provide a concrete design target for coverage-aware selection and context delivery.

</details>


### [210] [FINSKILLOPS: A Self-Evolving Multi-Agent System for SEC Filing QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19680)
*Yanzhang Ma,Zhenghan Tai,Hanwei Wu,Sizhe Guan,Jianliang Lei,Hailin He,Chaolong Jiang,Jijun Chi,Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Bohuai Xiao,Jingrui Tian,Xinlu Wu,Xingao Zhan,Peng Lu,Muzhi Li,Yihong Wu,Liheng Ma,Sicheng Lyu,Tianshuo Yan,Junhao Zhu,Yaqian Xu,Lei Ding,Yufei Cui,Ziquan Liu,Boyu Han,Hengli Liu,Ling Zhou,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将部署后改进建模为受控行为维护，通过作用域化技能补丁修复金融QA反复出现的错误而不引入回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融QA系统部署后可靠性行为固定，新SEC文件问题持续暴露期段、实体、证据使用与计算等异构错误；现有自改进方法难以控制修正作用范围，且可能破坏原有正确答案。

Method: 提出FINSKILLOPS多智能体系统，从证据锚定、类型化失败诊断中派生可复用技能，并通过定向验证、受保护用例回归检查、负对照以及版本化替换/退役进行治理。

Result: 在六个金融QA基准上，单一冻结技能注册表取得最高verdict加权正确率与参考一致性；增强基准上正确率从3.70提升至4.55；12轮运营研究中33个提议技能仅6个被采纳，监控非正确率由20.0%降至12.5%。

Conclusion: 受控的技能作用范围、准入机制与生命周期管理是实现可靠自改进的基础。

Abstract: Financial QA systems are typically improved before deployment through better retrieval, prompting, or agent coordination, leaving their reliability behavior fixed thereafter. In practice, new SEC-filing questions repeatedly expose heterogeneous errors in period, entity, evidence use, and calculation. Existing self-improvement methods can turn failures into new behaviors, but offer limited control over where a correction should apply or which previously correct answers it may break. We therefore frame post-deployment improvement as controlled behavioral maintenance: recurring failures should become scoped skill patches, and each patch should earn deployment with- out introducing regressions. We instantiate this view in FINSKILLOPS, a multi-agent system for SEC filing QA. FINSKILLOPS derives reusable skills from evidence-grounded, typed failure diagnoses and governs them through targeted validation, protected-case regression checks, negative controls, and versioned replacement or retirement. Across six financial QA benchmarks, a single frozen skill registry achieves the highest verdict-weighted correctness and reference consistency among the evaluated systems. Evolved skills raise correctness from 3.70 to 4.55 on our enhanced benchmark. In a separate 12-round operational study, only six of 33 proposed skills are promoted, while the monitoring non-correct rate falls from 20.0% to 12.5%. These results establish controlled skill scope, admission, and lifecycle management as the foundation for reliable self-improvement.

</details>


### [211] [A Unified Evaluation Framework for Trustworthy Large Language Models, Agentic AI, and Multimodal Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19524)
*Shaina Raza,Ahmed Y. Radwan,Imran Liaquat,Kathryn Hume*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的AI可信度评估框架，通过八个可信维度连接输出级、轨迹级和跨模态评估，并引入元评估层检验评估本身的有效性与可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠基准分数无法充分评估现代AI系统（LLM、智能体、多模态模型）的可信度，且不同类型系统需要不同评估方式，但其评估证据必须保持可解释性以支持开发与监管。

Method: 构建统一框架，通过八个可信维度（能力、鲁棒性、安全、公平、透明、治理、监督、效率）连接输出级、轨迹级和跨模态评估；保留系统特定指标并将其映射到共同性能带，附不确定性估计与可追溯证据；增加元评估层检验有效性与可复现性；引入安全关键覆盖机制防止聚合分数掩盖关键失败；并映射至治理框架、国际标准和欧盟法规。

Result: 框架可生成多维画像以揭示优势与弱点，防止聚合分数掩盖安全关键失败，并将技术评估与监管需求相连接；但跨部署情境的实证验证仍待完成。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估AI系统性能及支撑证据的可信度提供了结构化基础，实际部署中的实证验证是必要的下一步工作。

Abstract: Benchmark scores alone provide an incomplete basis for assessing the trustworthiness of modern artificial intelligence systems. Large language models (LLMs), agentic systems, and multimodal models (MLLMs) require different forms of assessment, yet their evaluation evidence must remain interpretable for development and oversight. We propose a unified framework that connects output-level, trajectory-level, and cross-modal assessment through eight trustworthiness dimensions: capability, robustness, safety, fairness, transparency, governance, oversight, and efficiency. The framework preserves system-specific metrics while mapping native measurements to common performance bands, accompanied by uncertainty estimates and traceable evidence. A meta-evaluation layer examines the validity, reliability, and reproducibility of the evaluation itself. Multidimensional profiles expose strengths and weaknesses, while safety-critical overrides prevent aggregate scores from masking critical failures. Mappings to governance frameworks, international standards, and European Union regulatory requirements connect technical assessment with oversight needs. The framework provides a structured basis for assessing both system performance and the credibility of the evidence supporting it, with empirical validation across deployment contexts remaining an essential next step.

</details>


### [212] [Q&A on Any Spreadsheet Requires Interpreting Its Grid Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20732)
*Zofia Smoleń*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于单元格角色标注的电子表格分块框架，改善LLM驱动的RAG可解释性，但指出离散分类存在天花板，主张转向降维技术将2D表格直接扁平化为1D文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格是二维非结构化数据，关系连续、单元格角色无限，而分类模型只能用有限预定义类别，难以完美捕捉结构细节，即使达到人类标注水平也存在硬上限。

Method: 提出将任意电子表格拆分为可解释块的框架，通过对单元格进行角色标注（语义单元格标注）来构建分块，从而为RAG提供更丰富的上下文。

Result: 该框架超越了现有最优方法，但受限于离散分类的固有瓶颈，无法完美表达表格的连续结构关系。语义标注主要提升了分块可解释性和答案生成，而非检索准确率。

Conclusion: 要突破电子表格到LLM的瓶颈，需超越离散单元格分类，发展降维技术，将2D非结构化电子表格直接扁平化为1D非结构化文本，便于下游RAG解释和生成。

Abstract: Semantic cell annotation improves chunking interpretability for spreadsheets in LLM-driven RAG systems, aiding answer generation through enriched context rather than improved retrieval accuracy. We propose a novel framework of splitting any spreadsheet into interpretable chunks using cell role annotation. Our framework beats the state of the art, yet it faces a hard ceiling. Spreadsheets are fundamentally two-dimensional unstructured data with continuous relationships and infinite potential cell roles. Because classification models are restricted to finite, pre-defined classes, they cannot perfectly capture this structural nuance, even with human-level annotation. We show that addressing the spreadsheet-to-LLM bottleneck requires moving beyond discrete cell classification. Instead, the field must develop dimensionality-reduction techniques to directly flatten 2D unstructured spreadsheets into 1D unstructured text. Text chunks would be easier for downstream RAG to interpret and generate from.

</details>


### [213] [Self Improvement via Fast Tree-search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19526)
*Xinghong Fu,Aravinth Kulanthaivelu,Yutaro Yamada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIFT是一个样本高效的编码智能体自我改进框架，用LLM裁判信号替代昂贵的基准评测，结合正则化Bradley-Terry模型和树搜索，在低资源下显著提升编码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体可递归修改自身实现形成自我改进循环，但现有方法依赖重跑基准任务子集来评估候选补丁，成本高、计算密集，难以在严格预算约束下使用。

Method: SIFT（Recursive Self Improvement via Fast Tree-search）：用LLM-as-a-judge对候选补丁做成对比较，胜-负记录通过正则化Bradley-Terry模型聚合得到强度分数，用于轻量解耦树搜索中的基于排名的父节点采样；昂贵的下游任务评测只保留给最有潜力的节点。

Result: 在完整Polyglot基准上，SIFT超过现有基于树搜索的自我进化框架，且在CPU小时、墙钟时间和API成本上资源需求显著更低。

Conclusion: 将候选自我修改的评估瓶颈从下游任务评测转移到LLM裁判信号，能实现样本高效的递归自我改进，在严格预算约束下兼顾性能与资源效率。

Abstract: Coding agents can recursively modify their own implementations, forming a loop of self-improvement. While prior work shows this can boost performance on coding benchmarks, existing approaches are costly and compute-intensive. We introduce a simple, sample-efficient self-improvement framework that significantly improves coding performance under strict budget constraints. We identify evaluation of candidate self-modifications as the main runtime bottleneck since prior approaches estimate their effectiveness by re-running a subset of benchmark tasks with the modified agent, which is time-consuming. We introduce Recursive Self Improvement via Fast Tree-search (SIFT), which augments these downstream task evaluations with an LLM-as-a-judge signal that performs pairwise comparisons between candidate patches, where the win-loss record is aggregated with a regularized Bradley-Terry model, and the resulting strength scores drive rank-based parent sampling inside a lightweight disaggregated tree search. Expensive downstream task evaluations are reserved only for the most promising nodes. Using a fully disaggregated tree search pipeline, the judge scores provide intermediate signal to guide exploration on promising candidate patches without being bottlenecked by slow evaluation runs. SIFT outperforms existing tree-search based self-evolution frameworks on the full Polyglot benchmark with significantly lower resource requirements in terms of CPU hours, wall clock time, and API cost.

</details>


### [214] [An Empirical Study of Harness Design for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20804)
*Run-Ze Fan,Zihao Zhang,Simin Ma,Yebowen Hu,Shouju Wang,Kaiqiang Song,Fei Liu,Hamed Zamani,Xiaoyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过模块化轻量编码框架，系统对比规划、动作空间与上下文管理三个组件在176种设置下的效果，揭示各组件在不同模型与预算下的作用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多将编码框架视为单体系统整体评估，无法判断各组件各自的贡献，因而难以指导组件级设计与选择。

Method: 固定执行循环的轻量编码框架，变化规划、动作空间、上下文管理三组件；在4个模型、SWE-Bench Verified与Terminal-Bench 2.1上评估176个匹配设置，覆盖5种上下文管理策略、4种上下文窗口预算，以及规划与动作空间的定向消融。

Result: 上下文预算越紧，上下文管理收益越大，主要来自避免溢出失败；规则式删除先于LLM摘要效率最佳，可恢复删除无增益；规划对弱模型提升准确率、对强模型主要降成本；预定义工具利好bash能力弱的模型，bash强的模型仅用bash接口更省成本。轨迹分析显示上下文管理延长轨迹，规划改变停止点，动作空间改变写码粒度。

Conclusion: 研究支持模型与预算感知的框架设计，并提供可模块化评估未来组件的框架。

Abstract: Coding harnesses shape how autonomous coding agents translate model capabilities into long-horizon software-engineering performance, yet existing work typically evaluates harnesses as monolithic systems, leaving the effectiveness of individual components unclear. To enable component-level comparisons, we study this question with a lightweight coding harness whose execution loop is fixed while three components are varied: planning, action space, and context management. Across four models evaluated on SWE-Bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.1, we evaluate 176 matched settings spanning five context-management strategies, four context-window budgets, and targeted ablations of planning and action space. We find that: (1) Context management becomes increasingly valuable as the context-window budget tightens, with most of its benefit coming from preventing context-overflow failures. (2) Staging rule-based elision before LLM-based summarization provides the strongest overall efficiency among the context-management strategies, whereas making elided content recoverable adds machinery that models rarely use and yields no accuracy gain. (3) Planning shifts from an accuracy scaffold for weaker models to a cost saver for stronger models, with little change in accuracy. (4) Predefined tools improve performance for models with weaker bash proficiency, whereas bash-capable models can operate effectively with a bash-only interface and achieve substantially lower cost, especially on command-line-centric tasks. Trajectory-level analysis explains these effects: context management extends execution trajectories without substantially altering agent behavior, planning changes where trajectories stop, and the action space changes the granularity at which code is written. These findings inform model- and budget-aware harness design and provide a modular framework for evaluating future harness components.

</details>


### [215] [When Hiring Becomes Agent-Mediated: Evaluating Access and Recurrence in Two-Agent Résumé Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19530)
*Jian Gao,Hang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将简历筛选建模为雇主侧与候选侧两个智能体交换证据并更新判断的双向流程，在600个构造的简历-岗位配对与191个边界样本上对比单次筛选。双智能体筛选整体提高通过率、改变决策方向并被单次阈值难以复现，且独有选中项在重复运行中复现稳定性更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 招聘本是双边过程：雇主评估匹配度，候选人呈现并维护自身资格。但作为第一道关卡的简历筛选常被简化为对"简历-岗位"配对的静态单次判定，忽略了候选人的申辩与互动，可能系统性影响谁能进入人工复核。

Method: 提出双智能体筛选：雇主侧智能体与候选侧智能体分别扮演双方角色，交换证据并在决定谁晋级前更新各自判断。在600个构造的简历-岗位配对上，使用GPT-5.5与Claude Opus 4.7比较单次筛选与双智能体筛选；并在共用的191个边界配对池上各运行三次。

Result: 双智能体筛选通过更多申请：GPT-5.5从33.3%升至39.3%，Opus 4.7从34.0%升至35.5%；在191个边界池三次运行中，逐实例通过率分别从4.5%升至26.2%、从6.5%升至16.1%。但并非一致放宽：它会拒绝一些单次筛选通过的申请，决策双向变化；在相近通过量下两者晋级对象不同，且没有任何单次阈值能稳定恢复双智能体筛选一致选中的申请。在新运行中复现的发现集里，仅双智能体选中的案例复现率低于共同选中案例（GPT-5.5明显，Opus 4.7较弱），而单独的"单次"跟进实验无类似下降。

Conclusion: 当招聘变成双侧智能体中介时，筛选"流程"本身（而不只是背后的模型）决定了谁进入人工复核以及这种机会复现的可靠性。

Abstract: Hiring is bilateral: employers assess fit, while candidates present and defend evidence of their qualifications. Yet résumé screening, the first gate, is commonly automated as a static, one-call judgment over a résumé-job pair. We study a two-agent alternative in which employer-side and candidate-side agents represent these roles, exchange evidence, and update their judgments before deciding who advances. We compare procedures on 600 constructed résumé-job pairs using GPT-5.5 and Claude Opus 4.7. Two-agent screening advances more applications (33.3% to 39.3% for GPT-5.5; 34.0% to 35.5% for Opus 4.7). Across three runs on the common 191-pair borderline pool, pass-instance rates rise from 4.5% to 26.2% and from 6.5% to 16.1%, respectively. This is not a uniform relaxation: two-agent screening rejects applications one-call advances, changing decisions in both directions. At similar pass volumes, the procedures advance different applications, and no one-call threshold recovers applications consistently selected by two-agent screening. Among discovery-selected cases re-executed in fresh runs, two-agent-only selections recur less often than shared selections, clearly under GPT-5.5 and less certainly under Opus 4.7, while a separate one-call follow-up shows no comparable decline. As hiring becomes agent-mediated on both sides, the screening procedure, not only the model behind it, shapes who reaches human review and how reliably that access recurs.

</details>


### [216] [Agentic AI Networking for Heterogeneous Unmanned Aerial Systems in Low-Altitude Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19538)
*Nguyen Duc Minh Quang,Chang Liu,Shuangyang Li,Derrick Wing Kwan Ng*
